# html5lib-tests' tokenizer tests

Generated by `scripts/html5lib_tokenizer.py` from html5lib-tests'
tokenizer tests, commit c777c408b610, under the MIT license (see
NOTICE at the top of the repo). Don't edit by hand.

One test in one initial state: its file, its description, the
input, the state's name as the test gives it, the last start tag
(or empty), and the expected tokens and errors in canonical form
(see `tokenizer-tests.temper.md`).

    export class TokenizerTest(
      public file: String,
      public description: String,
      public input: String,
      public state: String,
      public lastStartTag: String,
      public tokens: String,
      public errors: String,
    ) {}

All of them, as text in string literals parsed once, rather than as
thousands of calls each backend would compile. Every file but
namedEntities.test, which is in `named-entities.temper.md`.

    export let tokenizerTests(): List<TokenizerTest> {
      let all = new ListBuilder<TokenizerTest>();
      let rows = tokenizerTestsText().split("\u{E001}");
      for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i += 1) {
        let f = rows[i].split("\u{E002}");
        all.add(new TokenizerTest(f[0], f[1], f[2], f[3], f[4], f[5], f[6]));
      }
      all.toList()
    }
